def LogSobolev.entropy.{u_1} : {X : Type u_1} → [inst : MeasurableSpace X] → MeasureTheory.Measure X → (X → ℝ) → ℝ :=
fun {X} [MeasurableSpace X] μ g =>
  ∫ (x : X), g x * Real.log (g x) ∂μ - (∫ (x : X), g x ∂μ) * Real.log (∫ (x : X), g x ∂μ)
def LogSobolev.localSlope.{u_1} : {X : Type u_1} → [MetricSpace X] → (X → ℝ) → X → ENNReal :=
fun {X} [MetricSpace X] f x => Filter.limsup (fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (dist (f y) (f x) / dist y x)) (nhds x)
def LogSobolev.slopeEnergy.{u_1} : {X : Type u_1} →
  [MetricSpace X] → [inst : MeasurableSpace X] → MeasureTheory.Measure X → (X → ℝ) → ENNReal :=
fun {X} [MetricSpace X] [MeasurableSpace X] μ f => ∫⁻ (x : X), LogSobolev.localSlope f x ^ 2 ∂μ
def LogSobolev.MetricLSI.{u_1} : {X : Type u_1} →
  [MetricSpace X] → [inst : MeasurableSpace X] → MeasureTheory.Measure X → NNReal → Prop :=
fun {X} [MetricSpace X] [MeasurableSpace X] μ C =>
  ∀ (f : X → ℝ),
    LocallyLipschitz f →
      MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x => f x ^ 2) μ →
        MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x => f x ^ 2 * Real.log (f x ^ 2)) μ →
          ENNReal.ofReal (LogSobolev.entropy μ fun x => f x ^ 2) ≤ 2 * ↑C * LogSobolev.slopeEnergy μ f
def LogSobolev.unitaryHaar.{u_2} : (n : Type u_2) →
  [inst : Fintype n] → [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] → MeasureTheory.Measure ↥(Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ) :=
fun n [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n] => MeasureTheory.Measure.haarMeasure ⊤
def LogSobolev.phaseMap : (N : ℕ) →
  WithLp 2 (ℝ × ↥(Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup (Fin N) ℂ)) → ↥(Matrix.unitaryGroup (Fin N) ℂ) :=
fun N p => LogSobolev.phaseMul (√(2 / ↑N)) p.fst ⟨↑p.snd, ⋯⟩
def LogSobolev.intervalUniform : (L : ℝ) → MeasureTheory.Measure ↑(Set.Icc 0 L) :=
fun L => (ENNReal.ofReal L)⁻¹ • MeasureTheory.Measure.comap Subtype.val MeasureTheory.volume
def LogSobolev.phaseDomainMeasure : (N : ℕ) → MeasureTheory.Measure (LogSobolev.PhaseDomain N) :=
fun N =>
  MeasureTheory.Measure.map (WithLp.toLp 2)
    ((LogSobolev.intervalUniform (LogSobolev.phaseIntervalLength N)).prod (LogSobolev.specialUnitaryHaar (Fin N)))

  [inst : Fintype n] → [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] → LieSubalgebra ℝ (Matrix n n ℂ) :=
fun {n} [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n] =>
  { carrier := {A | star A = -A ∧ A.trace = 0}, add_mem' := ⋯, zero_mem' := ⋯, smul_mem' := ⋯, lie_mem' := ⋯ }
def LogSobolev.negTracePair.{u_1} : {n : Type u_1} → [Fintype n] → Matrix n n ℂ → Matrix n n ℂ → ℝ :=
fun {n} [Fintype n] A B => -(A * B).trace.re

                ⋯ ⋯)
